`topic-backlog.md` / `research-topics.md`.

`volume`/`kd` are the target keyword's Ahrefs metrics (copied at selection); the dashboard
computes Opportunity from them. `gemini` = Step-7 verdict (`human <hl>` / `ai <Y>`).
